vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-43-// According to the CL spec we are not allowed to let any cl_kernel object hold any references on
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-44-// its arguments as this might make it unfeasible for applications to free the backing memory of
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-45-// memory objects allocated with `CL_USE_HOST_PTR`.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-46-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-47-// However those arguments might temporarily get referenced by event objects, so we'll use Weak in
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-48-// order to upgrade the reference when needed. It's also safer to use Weak over raw pointers,
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-49-// because it makes it impossible to run into use-after-free issues.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-50-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-51-// Technically we also need to do it for samplers, but there it's kinda pointless to take a weak
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-52-// reference as samplers don't have the same host_ptr or any similar problems as cl_mem objects.

vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-43-// According to the CL spec we are not allowed to let any cl_kernel object hold any references on
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-44-// its arguments as this might make it unfeasible for applications to free the backing memory of
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-45-// memory objects allocated with `CL_USE_HOST_PTR`.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-46-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-47-// However those arguments might temporarily get referenced by event objects, so we'll use Weak in
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-48-// order to upgrade the reference when needed. It's also safer to use Weak over raw pointers,
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-49-// because it makes it impossible to run into use-after-free issues.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-50-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-51-// Technically we also need to do it for samplers, but there it's kinda pointless to take a weak
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-52-// reference as samplers don't have the same host_ptr or any similar problems as cl_mem objects.

vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-44-// According to the CL spec we are not allowed to let any cl_kernel object hold any references on
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-45-// its arguments as this might make it unfeasible for applications to free the backing memory of
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-46-// memory objects allocated with `CL_USE_HOST_PTR`.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-47-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-48-// However those arguments might temporarily get referenced by event objects, so we'll use Weak in
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-49-// order to upgrade the reference when needed. It's also safer to use Weak over raw pointers,
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-50-// because it makes it impossible to run into use-after-free issues.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-51-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-52-// Technically we also need to do it for samplers, but there it's kinda pointless to take a weak
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-53-// reference as samplers don't have the same host_ptr or any similar problems as cl_mem objects.

vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-44-// According to the CL spec we are not allowed to let any cl_kernel object hold any references on
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-45-// its arguments as this might make it unfeasible for applications to free the backing memory of
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-46-// memory objects allocated with `CL_USE_HOST_PTR`.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-47-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-48-// However those arguments might temporarily get referenced by event objects, so we'll use Weak in
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-49-// order to upgrade the reference when needed. It's also safer to use Weak over raw pointers,
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-50-// because it makes it impossible to run into use-after-free issues.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-51-//
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-52-// Technically we also need to do it for samplers, but there it's kinda pointless to take a weak
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/kernel.rs-53-// reference as samplers don't have the same host_ptr or any similar problems as cl_mem objects.
